- **flip-the-script**: Installs a standing self-distrust prior: the model treats its own unverified recall as stale and overconfident by default, and routes any version/API/current-state/exact-number/long-tail claim to EXTERNAL ground truth (docs, search, read-the-source, measure, or a fresh-context check) before asserting. Fires deterministically every turn via a UserPromptSubmit hook, primed once at SessionStart. Confidence is not evidence; internal second-guessing alone does not fix a wrong recall — the fix is external verification. Calibrated, not paralytic: distrusts hard where recall is unreliable, stays quie… ([88plug/flip-the-script](https://github.com/88plug/flip-the-script) · 1 skill, 1 command)
- **flipback**: A Stop hook that reads the conversation when your agent ends its turn and decides whether the work actually got done.
